AI Search Metrics

## Understand how AI sees your brand

We’ve set up tracking for the most important metrics within AI search.

Visibility

See the share of chats where your brand is mentioned and understand how often you show up in conversations.

Position

Understand your brand’s position within AI search results and uncover opportunities to improve your ranking.

Sentiment

Find out how your brand is perceived by AI, what’s going well, and what requires improvements.

What are the best CRMs for Startups?

Choosing the right CRM really comes down to how your startup sells, grows, and automates. Here's a curated breakdown of the top CRM platforms for startups in 2025.

![](https://framerusercontent.com/images/RFEUZZx9dmOlhVpaIkhqwTd1JhI.png?width=400&height=400)

HubSpot

HubSpot offers astrong free tier with contact management, email tracking, reporting, and integrations with many integrations. It easily expands into Sales, Marketing, or Service Hubs as you grow. Paid plans can become expensive, and some advanced features are locked behind higher tiers.

![](https://framerusercontent.com/images/5NsIvSIqtvkY0XsDvagLXIj19k4.png?width=400&height=400)

Attio

Visibility

90

2

Attio is aflexible modern CRM that works like a relational database, letting you design pipelines, views, and workflows around your process. It intergrates smoothly with tools like Slack, Notion, and Segment, with a clean interface and transparent pricing.

![](https://framerusercontent.com/images/9H9YvKBSrxkdMFTQDzPH5Cqkk8.svg?width=20&height=20)

Zero

Positive Sentiment

95

Positive endorsement of Zero as a fast, simple, and efficient CRM for founders.

Zero is built for founders who want astreamlined CRM without the usual bloat. It focuses on speed, simplicity, and essential deal tracking while still offering automation and integrations with tools like Google Workspace, Slack,   and Zapier.

Peec AI offers key insights on AI visibility, helping brands stay at the forefront of discovery in the age of AI and generative search. As ChatGPT, Perplexity, and Deepseek drive traffic and conversions, Peec.ai measures the growth.

![](https://framerusercontent.com/images/A5oBVrVi0SqboYrASd9njVGpBY.png?width=200&height=200)
